into the payload \"\n        \"(full gate lands in M4 T04.14).\"\n    ),\n)\n@click.option(\n    \"--output-dir\",\n    \"output_dir\",\n    type=click.Path(path_type=Path),\n    default=None,\n    help=(\n        \"Validate <path> against the OPS-002 / AC12 scratch-root policy. \"\n        \"A non-allowlisted root exits 2 and the stderr artifact quotes \"\n        \"the policy verbatim (see docs/eval/scratch-roots.md).\"\n    ),\n)\n@click.option(\n    \"--parallel\",\n    \"parallel\",\n    type=int,\n    default=None,\n    help=(\n        \"Declared concurrency for the next ``eval run``. When >= 15 the \"\n        \"doctor runs the NFR-PERF2 free-RAM precheck and warns on hosts \"\n        f\"with less than {RAM_CEILING_TEXT} free.\"\n    ),\n)\n@click.option(\n    \"--suite\",\n    \"suite\",\n    type=str,\n    default=None,\n    help=(\n        \"Suite name or manifest path used to resolve matcher coverage \"\n        \"when --check-coverage is set (FR-G5 / T04.14). Without --suite \"\n        \"the gate runs in matchers-only mode and reports every matcher \"\n        \"as uncovered.\"\n    ),\n)\ndef doctor(\n    as_json: bool,\n    no_mcp: bool,\n    check_coverage: bool,\n    output_dir: Optional[Path],\n    parallel: Optional[int],\n    suite: Optional[str],\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Verify host preconditions for ``superclaude eval run``.\n\n    Exit codes:\n\n    * ``0`` — every HARD capability satisfied.\n    * ``2`` — at least one HARD capability failed, or ``--output-dir``\n      was supplied and escaped the OPS-002 / AC12 scratch-root policy.\n      In both cases the stderr artifact identifies the cause; for a\n      scratch-root violation it quotes :data:`SCRATCH_ROOT_POLICY`\n      verbatim so operators can correct the invocation without\n      consulting the docs.\n    \"\"\"\n    if output_dir is not None:\n        try:\n            resolve_scratch_root(output_dir)\n        except ScratchRootViolation as exc:\n            click.echo(\n                f\"eval doctor: {format_scratch_root_violation(exc)}\",\n                err=True,\n            )\n            sys.exit(SCRATCH_ROOT_VIOLATION_EXIT_CODE)\n\n    skip_flags: tuple[str, ...] = (\"--no-mcp\",) if no_mcp else ()\n    report = build_doctor_report(\n        skip_flags=skip_flags,\n        requested_parallel=parallel,\n    )\n\n    coverage_result: Optional[CoverageResult] = None\n    if check_coverage:\n        suite_specs: tuple[EvalSpec, ...] = ()\n        if suite is not None:\n            try:\n                manifest_path = resolve_suite_manifest(suite, _DEFAULT_SUITES_DIR)\n                parsed = SuiteLoader().load(manifest_path)\n                suite_specs = parsed.evals\n            except SuiteNotFound as exc:\n                click.echo(f\"eval doctor: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}\", err=True)\n                sys.exit(SUITE_NOT_FOUND_EXIT_CODE)\n            except SuiteLoaderError as exc:\n                click.echo(\n                    f\"eval doctor: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}\", err=True\n                )\n                sys.exit(SUITE_LOADER_ERROR_EXIT_CODE)\n        coverage_result = coverage_gate(\n            settings_path=Path.home() / \".claude\" / \"settings.json\",\n            suite=suite_specs,\n            output_dir=None,\n        )\n\n    if as_json:\n        payload = doctor_payload(\n            report,\n            check_coverage=check_coverage,\n            coverage_result=coverage_result,\n        )\n        click.echo(json.dumps(payload, indent=2, sort_keys=True))\n    else:\n        click.echo(render_checklist(report))\n        if check_coverage and coverage_result is not None:\n            click.echo(_format_coverage_summary(coverage_result))\n\n    # NFR-PERF2 SOFT-SKIP warning: when ``--parallel >= 15`` was requested\n    # and the host could not certify the free-RAM budget, emit a stderr\n    # warning containing the literal :data:`RAM_CEILING_TEXT` token. The\n    # warning fires regardless of ``--json`` so CI logs and machine\n    # consumers see the same signal — the JSON payload still carries the\n    # row under ``soft_skips`` for structured parsing.\n    ram_row = next(\n        (r for r in report.report if r.name == \"host.free_ram_for_max_parallel\"),\n        None,\n    )\n    if ram_row is not None and not ram_row.passed:\n        click.echo(\n            f\"eval doctor: NFR-PERF2 warning: {ram_row.detail}\",\n            err=True,\n        )\n\n    if report.hard_failures:\n        click.echo(render_hard_failure_artifact(report), err=True)\n        sys.exit(HARD_FAIL_EXIT_CODE)\n\n    if coverage_result is not None and not coverage_result.passed:\n        click.echo(_format_coverage_missing_roster(coverage_result), err=True)\n        sys.exit(COVERAGE_GATE_FAILED_EXIT_CODE)\n\n\n@eval_group.command(\"list\")\n@click.option(\n    \"--json\",\n    \"as_json\",\n    is_flag=True,\n    help=\"Emit a JSON array of {name, version, eval_count} entries.\",\n)\n@click.option(\n    \"--suites-dir\",\n    \"suites_dir\",\n    type=click.Path(file_okay=False, path_type=Path),","numLines":160,"startLine":692,"totalLines":1647}}}

A missing path raises `SuiteNotFound` →\n   exit 2.\n3. **Loader validation** — `SuiteLoader().load(manifest_path)` runs FR-SCH1 (`suite.schema.json`)\n   and FR-SCH2 (`validate_eval_id` regex) across every authored eval row, expanding the E2\n   parameterize block into E2.1 / E2.2 / E2.3 in the process. Any schema or regex rejection\n   raises `SuiteLoaderError` → exit 2 BEFORE the coverage gate runs.\n4. **Coverage gate** — `coverage_gate(settings_path=~/.claude/settings.json, suite=<specs>, ...)`\n   crosswalks the loader's `EvalSpec` tuple against the hooks.json matcher patterns. A missing\n   matcher entry yields `CoverageResult.passed == False` → exit 2.\n5. **Renderer** — checklist + coverage summary printed to stdout; warnings (none in this run)\n   would go to stderr.\n\nA 0 exit therefore implies:\n\n- Every HARD capability is satisfied.\n- The `real` manifest exists and parses against `suite.schema.json`.\n- Every eval row (and every parameterize-expanded id) passes `validate_eval_id`.\n- The matcher-coverage gate is green for all three v1 matcher families.\n\n## Per-eval enumeration: doctor vs describe\n\nThe doctor command's primary stdout is the checklist; the per-eval roster is implicit in the\n`coverage_map` (only auggie-family ids surface there) plus the `SuiteLoader` return value\n(commands.py:788). The complete roster lives in `superclaude eval describe --suite real --json`,\nwhich is captured in `describe-ids.txt` for the AC \"All 15 evals (E1, E2.1-3, E3..E15) appear\nin the doctor output\". The describe output is the loader's projection of the same parsed\nmanifest the doctor consumed; both commands share `SuiteLoader().load(...)`.\n\n## What this DOES NOT prove\n\n- **Eval bodies pass `eval run`.** SC2 validates the manifest contract, not the runtime\n  outcome. End-to-end run-green is the M5 exit criterion (CP-P05-END / T05.28) and runs at\n  `--parallel 8` against the live PTY harness.\n- **Hook scripts emit the OQ-2-frozen ledger rows.** T05.07..T05.21 evidence notes the\n  pre-existing telemetry-emission gap (`logs/freshness.jsonl` vs the current scripts that\n  write to `logs/freshness-hook.jsonl` / `state/reads.jsonl` / `state/bg-agents/`). T05.22\n  is schema-coverage only; the script-side wiring is tracked separately.\n- **`eval describe` body equality.** The describe output is a snapshot of the validated\n  manifest; matching its content against D-0082 / D-0083..D-0099 happens per-eval in\n  T05.02..T05.21, not here.\n\n## Soft skips\n\nThree SOFT-SKIP rows appear in the checklist; none are schema violations:\n\n| Row | Reason | Impact on SC2 |\n|---|---|---|\n| `mcp_server.auggie-mcp` | binary not on PATH | none — capability gate is informational; `--no-mcp` would also skip. |\n| `mcp_server.airis-mcp-gateway` | binary not on PATH | none — same as above. |\n| `vendored.ptytest` | PTY vendor stub absent at `cli/eval/pty/__init__.py` | none — orthogonal to manifest validation; PTY landed in M2 / will land per T02.x. |\n\nThese are recorded in `sc2.log` for completeness; they do not gate SC2.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2645,"cache_read_input_tokens":144093,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2645,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":46,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available","speed":"standard"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"da234ed2-585c-4cd1-a1a0-983db9b15223","uuid":"710d3807-2fc5-49e0-a4ab-232632df5ab7"}
